with concerns of economic recession, which resulted in low consumer         業2022的疲態延續至2023年上半年。儘管如此，

    MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
    管理層討論及分析



    REVIEW OF OPERATIONS                                                           業務回顧

    The global economy exhibited different trends in the first and second          2023年全球經濟在上下半年展現出不同的態勢。
    half of 2023. In the first half of the year, global consumption contracted,    於上半年，由於全球經濟放緩、利率上行及通脹高
    the apparel industry performed unsatisfactorily, and customers became          踞不下，導致環球消費收縮，服裝行業表現未如理
    more cautious in placing orders due to the global economic slowdown,           想，客戶下單亦較為謹慎。其中，美國紡織品及服
    rising interest rates and high inflation. In particular, imports of textiles   飾進口在首半年大幅下降22.7%。然而，下半年起
    and apparel from the United States dropped significantly by 22.7% in           全球市場逐漸企穩，直至第四季度更見好轉趨勢，
    the first half of the year. However, the global market gradually stabilized    各地零售業開始呈現復甦勢頭。根據中國服裝協會
    in the second half of the year, with the fourth quarter showing signs of       網，以中國服裝出口為例，新年等節日消費需求提
    improvement, and retailers around the world began to see the strength          升及美國去庫存見成效等因素推動下，2023年11

    Association, China’s apparel exports improved significantly in November        穩中求勝，憑藉龐大銷售網絡及充足產能的全球佈
    and December 2023, driven by factors such as increased consumer                局，除了抵禦個別巿場表現波動的風險，也能迅速
    demand during festive seasons such as the New Year and the effect              回應市場需求，抓住市場復甦的先機。
    of destocking in the United States. During the year, the Group strived

Reasons for the change in use of proceeds                                      更改所得款項用途之理由

As disclosed in the announcement of the Company dated 18 October               誠如本公司日期為2023年10月18日內容有關新孟
2023 in relation to the completion of construction of the New Bangladesh       加拉廠房完成建設的公告所示，本集團已完成建造
Factory, the Group has completed construction of the New Bangladesh            新孟加拉廠房。由於建築成本整體下降，故建築實
Factory. The actual costs of the construction have been less than              際成本較預計成本為低。此外，董事會認為，為新
the anticipated costs as construction costs in general have reduced.           孟加拉廠房額外購買機器未必能為本集團業務帶來
Moreover, the Board considers that further purchase of machinery for           突破，亦未必可為本集團創造更佳的財務業績和回
the New Bangladesh Factory may not be able to make a breakthrough              報，原因為基於近期經濟呈衰退跡象、通脹及利率
in the Group’s business and generate better financial results and return       持續上升影響購買力，而商品需求疲弱使存貨逐步
for the Group, due to the recent downturn of the economy, inflation            增加，導致採購訂單大幅下降。因此，董事會認
and interest rate hikes affected purchasing power, and inventories had         為，本集團在執行擴充計劃方面應加倍審慎。
been steadily increasing under the low demand for goods, resulting in
a significant decrease in purchase orders. As such, the Board considers
that a more cautious approach in relation to the execution of the Group’s
expansion plan should be adopted.

In light of the above, the Board considers that the reallocation of the        基於上文所述，董事會相信，將未動用所得款項淨
remaining unutilised net proceeds from the construction of the New             額餘額用途由建造新孟加拉廠房及為新孟加拉廠房
Bangladesh Factory and purchasing machinery for the New Bangladesh             購買機器重新分配至一般營運資金，此舉有助本集
Factory to the use as general working capital would allow the Group to         團能更靈活、更有效使用其財務資源，對本集團更
utilise its financial resources in a more flexible, beneficial and effective   為有利。另外，董事會認為，重新分配未動用所得
way. The Board is also of the view that the reallocation will allow the        款項淨額之用途將可滿足本集團的營運需要，為應
Group to meet its operational needs and provide more buffer to cope            對未來經濟不確定因素提供更大的緩衝保護。
with the economic uncertainties in the future.

The Board confirms that there are no material changes in the nature of         董事會確認，本集團業務性質並無重大變動，且認
the business of the Group and considered that the proposed change is in        為上述建議變動符合本公司及其股東的整體利益，
the interests of the Company and its shareholders as a whole and will not      不會對本集團現有業務及營運構成任何重大不利影
have any material adverse effect on the existing business and operations       響。
of the Group.

Save as the changes disclosed above, there are no other proposed               除上文所披露的變動外，所得款項淨額的用途概無
changes in the use of the Net Proceeds. The Unutilised Net Proceeds will       其他建議變動。本公司將按照上述擬訂用途動用未
be applied in a manner consistent with the above planned applications.         使用所得款項淨額。

     CODE ON CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                     企業管治常規守則

     The Company is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate        本公司致力保持高標準的企業管治，以保障其股東
     governance to protect the interests of its Shareholders and to enhance     利益，提升公司價值及問責性。董事會已檢討本公
     corporate value and accountability. The Board has reviewed the             司企業管治慣例，並信納本公司於報告期間已遵守
     Company’s corporate governance practices and is satisfied that the         聯交所證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄C1所載企
     Company has complied with all Code Provisions of the Corporate             業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）的所有守則條文。
     Governance Code (“CG Code”) as set out in Appendix C1 to the Rules         本公司已於本報告所述之企業管治結構及慣例中套
     Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the “Listing    用企業管治守則的原則，並將繼續提升適用於其業
     Rules”) during the Reporting Period. The Company has applied the           務操守及增長的企業管治慣例，並不時檢討有關慣
     principles of the CG Code to its corporate governance structure and        例，確保其符合企業管治守則並與最新發展一致。
     practices as described in this report. The Company will continue to

     latest developments.

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTIONS                                             企業管治職責

     The Board is responsible for performing the corporate governance duties    董事會負責履行企業管治守則守則條文第A.2.1條
     set out in Code Provision A.2.1 of the CG Code. The Board recognises       所載的企業管治職責。董事會深知企業管治為董事
     that corporate governance should be the collective responsibility of the   的共同責任，彼等的職責包括：

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                          風險管理及內部監控

The Company places great importance on internal control and risk              本公司非常重視內部監控及風險管理，以保障本公
management to safeguard the assets of the Company and the interests           司資產及股東利益。董事會全面負責內部監控制
of Shareholders. The Board has overall responsibility for the system of       度，並檢討其成效。審核委員會協助董事會履行其
internal control and for reviewing its effectiveness. The Audit Committee     維持有效內部監控制度之責任。審核委員會檢討本
assists the Board in meeting its responsibilities for maintaining an          集團評估其監控環境之程序及風險評估程序，以及
effective system of internal control. The Audit Committee reviews the         透過本公司內部監控顧問之協助管理業務及監控風
process by which the Group evaluates its control environment and risk         險之方法。
assessment process, and the way in which business and control risks
are managed through the assistance of the Company’s Internal Control
Consultant.

The Internal Control Consultant assesses risk exposure, formulates audit      內部監控顧問評估風險，制訂審核計劃及確保審核
plan and ensures that the audit programs cover key internal control areas     程序以輪替方式涵蓋營運附屬公司之關鍵內部監控
of operating subsidiaries on a rotational basis for the review by the Audit   範圍，以供審核委員會審閱。
Committee.

The Internal Control Consultant reports their findings and recommendations    內部監控顧問向本公司管理層及審核委員會匯報彼
to management of the Company and the Audit Committee. The Internal            發現事項及建議。內部監控顧問亦監察因應彼建議
Control Consultant also monitors the follow- up actions agreed upon in        而協定之跟進行動，並向審核委員會報告該等建議
response to her recommendations and reports to the Audit Committee            之實施進度。
the progress of implementation of those recommendations.

With the assistance of the Audit Committee and the Company’s Internal         在審核委員會及本公司內部監控顧問協助下，董事
Control Consultant, the Board is satisfied that the overall financial,        會信納本集團的整體財務、營運及合規監控以及風
operational and compliance controls, and risk management of the Group         險管理仍然有效充足。
continues to be effective and adequate.

     AUDITOR’S REMUNERATION                                                     核數師酬金

     For the year ended 31 December 2023, the auditors of the Company           截 至 2023 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度 ， 本 公 司 核 數 師
     received approximately HK$2,160,000 (2022: HK$2,200,000) for their         就 其 核 數 服 務 收 取 約 2,160,000 港 元（ 2022 年 ：
     audit service. Non-audit service which covered taxation service provided   2,200,000港元）。同年，其向本集團提供之非核數

    meetings. Such requisition shall be made in writing to the Board or           形式向董事會或公司秘書提出，藉以要求董事

    such requisition and such meeting shall be held within two months             個月內舉行；

    Shareholders concerned, specify the objects of the meeting, and               會的事由，並由相關合資格股東簽署。合資格
    be signed by the Eligible Shareholders concerned. The Eligible                股東必須證明彼等於本公司的持股量足以令本

     OPINION                                                                       意見

     We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Cirtek               吾等已審核列載於第52至156頁的常達控股有限公
     Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”)           司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴集團」）的綜合財務

be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such
communication.

     2.1 Basis of preparation                                                          2.1 編製基準

         These financial statements have been prepared in accordance                     該等財務報表乃按照香港會計師公會（「香
         with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”)                         港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務報告準則
         (which include all HKFRSs, Hong Kong Accounting Standards                      （「香港財務報告準則」）（ 包括所有香港財
         (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong                          務報告準則、香港會計準則（「香港會計準
         Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and                        則」）及詮釋）及香港公司條例的披露規定
         the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies                          編製。
         Ordinance.

         These financial statements have been prepared under the                        該等財務報表乃按歷史成本法編製，惟按
         historical cost convention, except for a financial asset at fair               公平值計入損益的金融資產則按公平值計
         value through profit or loss which has been measured at fair                   量。該等財務報表以港元列值，除另有
         value. These financial statements are presented in Hong Kong                   指明外，所有數值已約整至最接近的千位
         dollars and all values are rounded to the nearest thousand                     （千港元）。
         (HK$’000) except when otherwise indicated.

         Basis of consolidation                                                          綜合入賬基準

         The consolidated financial statements include the financial                     綜合財務報表包括本集團截至2023年12
         statements of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2023.                    月31日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司指由
         A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly or          本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結構
         indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when                 性實體）。當本集團通過參與投資對象的
         the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its               相關活動而承擔可變回報的風險或有權享
         involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect those               有可變回報，並且有能力透過對投資對象
         returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights              的權力（即賦予本集團現有能力以主導投
         that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant                  資對象的相關活動的現有權利）影響該等
         activities of the investee).                                                    回報時，即表示擁有控制權。

         Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights              一般而言，推定持有大部分投票權者擁有
         results in control. When the Company has less than a majority of                控制權。當本公司擁有少於投資對象大多
         the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers                數投票權或類似權利，則本集團於評估其
         all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether it has                對投資對象是否擁有權力時考慮一切相關
         power over an investee, including:                                              事實及情況，包括：

         (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of                  (a) 與投資對象其他投票權持有人的合約
             the investee;                                                                   安排；

         (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                     (b) 來自其他合約安排的權利；及

         (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                      (c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

         The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for                   附屬公司的財務報表乃按與本公司於同一
         the same reporting period as the Company, using consistent                      報告期間編製，並使用一致的會計政策。
         accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated               附屬公司之業績自本集團取得控制權當日
         from the date on which the Group obtains control, and continue                  起綜合入賬，並持續綜合入賬直至該控制
         to be consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                     權終止當日止。

         Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                        損益及其他全面收益的各組成部分屬本集
         income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group                  團母公司擁有人及非控股權益的應佔部
         and to the non-controlling interests, even if this results in the               分，即使此將導致非控股權益出現虧絀結
         non-controlling interests having a deficit balance. All intra-group             餘。所有有關與本集團股東之間交易的集
         assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash flows                 團內資產及負債、權益、收入、開支及現
         relating to transactions between shareholders of the Group are                  金流量均於綜合入賬時全面對銷。
         eliminated in full on consolidation.

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                             3.   重要會計政策

     Investment in an associate                                                    於一間聯營公司的投資

     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term interest         聯 營 公 司 為 本 集 團 於 其 持 有 一 般 附 帶 不少於
     of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights and over           20%表決權的長期股本權益，並具有重大影響
     which it has significant influence. Significant influence is the power        力的實體。重大影響力指有權參與投資對象的
     to participate in the financial and operating policy decisions of the         財務及經營政策決定，惟並非控制或共同控制
     investee, but is not control or joint control over those policies.            該等政策。

     The Group’s investment in an associate is stated in the consolidated          本集團於一間聯營公司的投資乃按本集團根據
     statement of financial position at the Group’s share of net assets            權益會計法應佔資產淨值減任何減值虧損於綜
     under the equity method of accounting, less any impairment                    合財務狀況表內列賬。本集團應佔聯營公司收
     losses. The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and                 購後業績及其他全面收益分別計入綜合損益表
     other comprehensive income of associates is included in the                   及綜合其他全面收益表。此外，倘於聯營公司
     consolidated statement of profit or loss and consolidated other               直接確認的權益出現變動，則本集團將於綜合
     comprehensive income, respectively. In addition, when there has               權益變動表內確認其應佔任何變動（如適用）。
     been a change recognised directly in the equity of the associate,             本集團與其聯營公司之間交易的未變現收益及
     the Group recognises its share of any changes, when applicable, in            虧損將以本集團於聯營公司的投資為限對銷，
     the consolidated statement of changes in equity. Unrealised gains             惟 未 變 現 虧 損 提 供 所 轉 讓 資 產 減 值 的 憑證除
     and losses resulting from transactions between the Group and its              外。收購該聯營公司所產生的商譽計入本集團
     associate are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s investment              於聯營公司的投資一部分。
     in the associate, except where unrealised losses provide evidence
     of an impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising from
     the acquisition of the associate is included as part of the Group’s
     investment in an associate.

     If an investment in an associate becomes an investment in a joint             倘於聯營公司的投資成為於合營企業的投資或
     venture or vice versa, the retained interest is not remeasured.               出現相反情況，保留權益將不予重新計量。反
     Instead, the investment continues to be accounted for under the               之，該投資繼續根據權益法入賬。在所有其他
     equity method. In all other case, upon loss of significant influence          情況下，於失去對聯營公司的重大影響力或失
     over the associate or joint control over the joint venture, the Group         去對合營企業的共同控制權時，本集團按其公
     measures and recognises any retained investment at its fair value.            平值計量及確認任何保留投資。於失去重大影
     Any difference between the carrying amount of the associate or joint          響力或共同控制權時，聯營公司或合營企業的
     venture upon loss of significant influence or joint control and the           賬面值與保留投資及出售所得款項的公平值之
     fair value of the retained investment and proceeds from disposal is           間的任何差額於損益中確認。
     recognised in profit or loss.

          Business combinations and goodwill                                            業務合併及商譽

          Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition                 業務合併使用收購法入賬。轉讓代價按收購日
          method. The consideration transferred is measured at the acquisition          期公平值計量，即本集團所轉讓資產、本集團
          date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition date fair values          承擔的被收購方前擁有人負債與本集團為換取
          of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group          被收購方控股權而發行的股本權益於收購日期
          to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity interests                 公平值的總和。就各項業務合併而言，本集團
          issued by the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For              選擇是否按公平值或佔被收購方的可識別淨資
          each business combination, the Group elects whether to measure                產的比例計量於被收購方的非控股權益。非控
          the non-controlling interests in the acquiree at fair value or at the         股權益的所有其他部分按公平值計量。收購相
          proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All            關成本於產生時支銷。
          other components of non-controlling interests are measured at fair
          value. Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.

          The Group determines that it has acquired a business when                     當本集團收購一組業務及資產，而當中包括共
          the acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and a             同對創造產出的能力具有重大貢獻的一項投入
          substantive process that together significantly contribute to the             和實質性過程時，則確定其已收購一項業務。
          ability to create outputs.

          When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial assets          當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約條款、
          and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and designation        於收購日期的經濟狀況及相關條件評估所承擔
          in accordance with the contractual terms, economic circumstances              的金融資產及負債，以作出適當分類及指定，
          and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes            包括將被收購方所訂立的主合約中的嵌入式衍
          the separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the               生工具拆分。
          acquiree.

          If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously             倘業務合併分階段進行，則先前持有的股權按
          held equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition date fair value         其於收購日期的公平值重新計量，而任何所導
          and any resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.               致的收益或虧損乃於損益確認。

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                  3.   重要會計政策
                                                                                         （續）

     Business combinations and goodwill (Continued)                                 業務合併及商譽
                                                                                          （續）

     Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer                 將由收購方轉讓的任何或然代價按於收購日期
     is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent                的公平值確認。分類為資產或負債的或然代價
     consideration classified as an asset or a liability is measured                按公平值計量且公平值變動於損益確認。分類
     at fair value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or               為權益的或然代價不會重新計量，而其後續結
     loss. Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is not             算於權益內入賬。
     remeasured and subsequent settlement is accounted for within
     equity.

     Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of the                商譽初步按成本計量，即已轉讓代價、已確認
     aggregate of the consideration transferred, the amount recognised              非控股權益金額及本集團先前持有的被收購方
     for non-controlling interests and any fair value of the Group’s                股權的任何公平值之總和超出所收購可識別資
     previously held equity interests in the acquiree over the identifiable         產及所承擔負債的差額。倘此代價及其他項目
     assets acquired and liabilities assumed. If the sum of this                    的總和低於所收購淨資產的公平值，則其差額
     consideration and other items is lower than the fair value of the net          將於重新評估後於損益確認為議價收購收益。
     assets acquired, the difference is, after reassessment, recognised in
     profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.

     After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less any               於初步確認後，商譽按成本減任何累計減值虧
     accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for impairment               損計量。商譽每年會作減值測試，或倘有事件
     annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances              或情況變動顯示賬面值可能出現減值，則會更
     indicate that the carrying value may be impaired. The Group                    頻密地進行測試。本集團於12月31日對商譽
     performs its annual impairment test of goodwill as at 31 December.             進行年度減值測試。就減值測試而言，於業務
     For the purpose of impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a                  合併中收購的商譽會自收購日期起分配至預期
     business combination is, from the acquisition date, allocated to each          可從合併產生的協同效應中獲益的本集團各現
     of the Group’s cash-generating units, or groups of cash-generating             金產生單位或現金產生單位組別，而不論本集
     units, that are expected to benefit from the synergies of the                  團其他資產或負債是否分配至有關單位或單位
     combination, irrespective of whether other assets or liabilities of the        組別。
     Group are assigned to those units or groups of units.

     Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable amount of                減值按與商譽有關的現金產生單位（現金產生
     the cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating units) to which             單位組別）的可收回金額的評估釐定。倘現金
     the goodwill relates. Where the recoverable amount of the cash-                產生單位（現金產生單位組別）的可收回金額低
     generating unit (group of cash-generating units) is less than the              於賬面值，則確認減值虧損。已確認的商譽減
     carrying amount, an impairment loss is recognised. An impairment               值虧損不會於後續期間撥回。
     loss recognised for goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent period.

          Fair value measurement                                                            公平值計量

          The Group measures its financial asset at fair value through profit or            本集團於各報告期末計量其按公平值計入損益
          loss at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that            的金融資產。公平值指於計量日期市場參與者
          would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an          之間的有序交易中為出售資產而收取或轉讓負
          orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement                債而支付的價格。公平值計量乃基於出售資產
          date. The fair value measurement is based on the presumption                      或轉讓負債之交易於該資產或負債之主要市場
          that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes            或（倘並無主要市場）就該資產或負債而言最具
          place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the        優勢的市場進行之假設。主要或最具優勢市場
          absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous market for                須為本集團可進入的市場。資產或負債的公平
          the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market             值乃使用市場參與者將資產或負債定價時使用
          must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a                  的假設計量（假設市場參與者依照彼等的最佳
          liability is measured using the assumptions that market participants              經濟利益行事）。
          would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market
          participants act in their economic best interest.

          A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account              非金融資產的公平值計量考慮市場參與者於資
          a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using             產最高及最佳使用狀態時使用或將其出售予另
          the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market          一個市場參與者所產生經濟利益能力。
          participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.

          The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                   本集團使用適用於有關情況且具備充足可供使
          circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure              用數據以計量公平值的估值技術，以盡可能使
          fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and                  用相關可觀察輸入值及盡可能減少使用不可觀
          minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                        察輸入值。

          All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or disclosed          於財務報表計量或披露公平值的所有資產及負
          in the financial statements are categorised within the fair value                 債均根據對公平值計量整體而言屬重大的最低
          hierarchy, described as follows, based on the lowest level input that             層輸入值按下述公平值層級進行分類：
          is significant to the fair value measurement as a whole:

          Level 1 –     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets               第一級   － 基於相同資產或負債於活躍市場
                        for identical assets or liabilities                                         的報價（未經調整）

          Level 2 –     based on valuation techniques for which the lowest                  第二級   － 基於可直接或間接觀察就公平值
                        level input that is significant to the fair value                           計量而言屬重大的最低層輸入值
                        measurement is observable, either directly or indirectly                    的估值技術

          Level 3 –     based on valuation techniques for which the lowest                  第三級   － 基於無法觀察就公平值計量而言
                        level input that is significant to the fair value                           屬重大的最低層輸入值的估值技
                        measurement is unobservable                                                 術

     Impairment of non-financial assets                                             非金融資產減值

     Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual                       倘有跡象顯示存在減值，或當需要對一項資產
     impairment testing for an asset is required (other than inventories            （存貨及遞延稅項資產除外）作年度減值測試，
     and deferred tax assets), the asset’s recoverable amount is                    則估計資產之可收回金額。資產之可收回金額
     estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is the higher of the                  為資產或現金產生單位之使用價值以及其公平
     asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value in use and its fair value              值減出售成本之較高者計算，並就個別資產而
     less costs of disposal, and is determined for an individual asset,             釐定，惟倘資產並未能產生大致獨立於其他資
     unless the asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely               產或資產組別的現金流入，則就資產所屬之現
     independent of those from other assets or groups of assets, in which           金產生單位釐定可收回金額。
     case the recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating
     unit to which the asset belongs.

     An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount of an             減值虧損僅於資產之賬面值超過其可收回金額
     asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in use,               時確認。於評估使用價值時，估計未來現金流
     the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present                量乃採用反映當時市場對貨幣時間價值以及資
     value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market               產特定風險的除稅前貼現率貼現至其現值。減
     assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific to the           值虧損按與該減值資產之功能相符的開支類別
     asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or loss in the period           於產生期間自損益扣除。
     in which it arises in those expense categories consistent with the
     function of the impaired asset.

     An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as to                於 各 報 告 期 末 須 評 估 是 否 有 跡 象 顯 示 先前確
     whether there is an indication that previously recognised impairment           認之減值虧損可能不再存在或可能已減少。倘
     losses may no longer exist or may have decreased. If such an                   存在有關跡象，則對可收回金額作出估計。先
     indication exists, the recoverable amount is estimated. A previously           前確認的資產（商譽除外）減值虧損僅於用以
     recognised impairment loss of an asset other than goodwill is                  釐定資產之可收回金額之估計出現變動時方予
     reversed only if there has been a change in the estimates used                 撥回，惟有關金額不得超過倘有關資產於過往
     to determine the recoverable amount of that asset, but not to an               年度並未有確認減值虧損而將已釐定之賬面值
     amount higher than the carrying amount that would have been                    （扣除任何折舊╱攤銷）。有關減值虧損的撥回
     determined (net of any depreciation/amortisation) had no impairment            計入產生當期的損益。
     loss been recognised for the asset in prior years. A reversal of such
     an impairment loss is credited to profit or loss in the period in which
     it arises.

     while financial assets classified and measured at fair value through          金流量及出售金融資產。並非按上述業務模式

          a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss        險而言，不論何時發生違約，於餘下風險年期

          recognition. When making the assessment, the Group compares                    於報告日期金融工具發生違約的風險與於初步

          information. The Group considers that there has been a significant

     Simplified approach                                                               簡化方式

     For trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing                 就不包括重大融資部分的貿易應收款項而言，
     component or when the Group applies the practical expedient of                    或當本集團應用可行權宜方法不就重大融資部
     not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component, the                分的影響作出調整時，本集團採用簡化方式計
     Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the              算預期信貸虧損。根據簡化方式，本集團不會
     simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in credit risk,             追蹤信貸風險變動，惟將根據全期預期信貸虧
     but instead recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at                 損確認各報告日期的虧損撥備。本集團已根據
     each reporting date. The Group has established a provision matrix                 其過往信貸虧損經驗訂立撥備矩陣，並按債務
     that is based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted for              人及經濟環境具體的前瞻性因素作出調整。
     forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic
     environment.

     For trade receivables that contain a significant financing component              就包括重大融資組成部分的貿易應收款項及租
     and lease receivables, the Group chooses as its accounting policy                 賃應收款項而言，本集團採納簡化方式為其會
     to adopt the simplified approach in calculating ECLs with policies as             計政策，並按上述政策計算預期信貸虧損。
     described above.

          Financial liabilities                                                             金融負債

          Initial recognition and measurement                                               初步確認及計量

          Financial liabilities are classified, at initial recognition, as financial        金融負債於初步確認時分類為按公平值計入損
          liabilities at fair value through profit or loss, loans and borrowings,           益的金融負債、貸款及借款、應付款項或指定
          payables, or as derivatives designated as hedging instruments in an               作有效對沖的對沖工具之衍生工具（視情況而
          effective hedge, as appropriate.                                                  定）。

          All financial liabilities are recognised initially at fair value and, in          所有金融負債初步按公平值確認。倘為貸款及
          the case of loans and borrowings and payables, net of directly                    借款以及應付款項，則須扣除直接應佔交易成
          attributable transaction costs.                                                   本。

          The Group’s financial liabilities include trade payables, financial               本集團的金融負債包括貿易應付款項、計入其
          liabilities included in other payables and accruals, lease liabilities and        他應付款項及應計費用的金融負債、租賃負債
          interest-bearing bank borrowings.                                                 及計息銀行借款。

          Subsequent measurement                                                            後續計量

          The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities depends on their              金 融 負 債 的 後 續 計 量 乃 視 乎 下 文 所 述 分 類而
          classification as follows:                                                        定：

          Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other payables, and            按攤銷成本計量的金融負債（貿易及其他應付
          borrowings)                                                                       款項以及借款）

          After initial recognition, trade and other payables, and interest-                於初步確認後，貿易及其他應付款項以及計息
          bearing borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised                         借款隨後以實際利率法按攤銷成本計量，惟倘
          cost, using the effective interest rate method unless the effect of               貼現影響屬不重大，則按成本列賬。當負債終
          discounting would be immaterial, in which case they are stated at                 止確認及按實際利率進行攤銷程序時，其收益
          cost. Gains and losses are recognised in the statement of profit or               及虧損於損益表內確認。
          loss when the liabilities are derecognised as well as through the
          effective interest rate amortisation process.

          Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount or               攤銷成本乃經考慮收購的任何折讓或溢價，以
          premium on acquisition and fees or costs that are an integral part of             及屬實際利率組成部分的費用或成本後計算。
          the effective interest rate. The effective interest rate amortisation is          實際利率攤銷於損益表入賬列作融資成本。
          included in finance costs in the statement of profit or loss.

          Derecognition of financial liabilities                                            終止確認金融負債

          A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the               當金融負債項下的責任被解除或取消或到期，
          liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                                 則終止確認金融負債。

          When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the              倘現有金融負債由同一貸款人授予條款顯著不
          same lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an                  同的另一項負債取代，或現有負債的條款經重
          existing liability are substantially modified, such an exchange or                大修訂，則有關交換或修訂視作終止確認原有
          modification is treated as a derecognition of the original liability              負債及確認新負債，其賬面值的差額於損益表
          and a recognition of a new liability, and the difference between the              確認。
          respective carrying amounts is recognised in the statement of profit
          or loss.

     are readily convertible into known amounts of cash, subject to an                 擔 的 一 般 於 三 個 月 內 到 期 的 短 期 高 流 動性存

     Provisions                                                                        撥備

     constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is probable           後可能須動用資源履行有關責任，且有關責任

     obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the

     or loss.

               customer. To estimate the variable consideration for the                       的可變代價而言，單一批量限額的合
               expected future rebates, the most likely amount method is                      約乃使用最可能金額方法，而多於一
               used for contracts with a single-volume threshold and the                      項批量限額的合約則使用預期價值方
               expected value method for contracts with more than one                         法。所選擇的方法能最佳預計可變代
               volume threshold. The selected method that best predicts                       價金額，並主要由合約所載批量限額
               the amount of variable consideration is primarily driven by                    數字所帶動。已應用有關限制可變代

     4.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND ESTIMATES                            4.   重大會計判斷及估計

          The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires                    管理層編製本集團的財務報表時須作出判斷、
          management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions that                   估計及假設，而該等判斷、估計及假設會影響
          affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets and                   所申報的收益、開支、資產及負債的金額以及
          liabilities, and their accompanying disclosures, and the disclosure             相關披露及或然負債的披露。有關此等假設及
          of contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these assumptions and              估計的不確定因素可能產生的結果或會導致未
          estimates could result in outcomes that could require a material                來受影響的資產或負債賬面金額須作出重大調
          adjustment to the carrying amounts of the assets or liabilities                 整。
          affected in the future.

          Judgement                                                                       判斷

          In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,                     於應用本集團會計政策的過程中，除涉及估計
          management has made the following judgement, apart from those                   的判斷外，管理層已作出以下對財務報表確認
          involving estimations, which have the most significant effect on the            的金額構成最重大影響的判斷：
          amounts recognised in the financial statements:

          Determining the method to estimate variable consideration and                   釐定估計可變代價的方法及評估出售印刷產品
          assessing the constraint for the sale of printing products                      的限制

          Certain contracts for the sale of printing products include a right             若干出售印刷產品的合約包括產生可變代價的
          of return that give rises to variable consideration. In estimating              退貨權利。於估計可變代價時，本集團須視乎
          the variable consideration, the Group is required to use either the             何種方法可更能預測其有權收取的代價金額，
          expected value method or the most likely amount method based on                 以決定使用預期價值法或最可能金額法。
          which method better predicts the amount of consideration to which
          it will be entitled.

          The Group determined that the most likely amount method is the                  本 集 團 確 認 ， 鑒 於 有 大 量 特 徵 類 似 的 客 戶合
          appropriate method to use in estimating the variable consideration              約，最可能金額法為估計出售有退貨權的印刷
          for the sale of printing products with rights of return, given the large        產品之可變代價時使用的適當方法。
          number of customer contracts that have similar characteristics.

          Before including any amount of variable consideration in the                    將任何可變代價金額計入交易價格前，本集團
          transaction price, the Group considers whether the amount of                    會考慮可變代價金額是否受到限制。本集團根
          variable consideration is constrained. The Group determined that the            據其過往經驗、業務預測及當前經濟狀況釐定
          estimates of variable consideration are not constrained based on its            可變代價的估計並無受到限制。此外，可變代
          historical experience, business forecast and the current economic               價的不確定因素將於短時間內解決。
          conditions. In addition, the uncertainty on the variable consideration
          will be resolved within a short time frame.

     Provision for expected credit losses on trade receivables                      貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損撥備

     The Group uses a provision matrix to calculate ECLs for trade                  本集團使用撥備矩陣計量貿易應收款項的預期
     receivables. The provision rates are based on days past due for                信貸虧損。撥備率基於具有類似虧損模式（即
     groupings of various customer segments that have similar loss                  按地理位置）各客戶群分部的逾期日數。
     patterns (i.e., by geography).

     The provision matrix is initially based on the Group’s historical              撥備矩陣初步以本集團的歷史可觀察違約率為
     observed default rates. The Group will calibrate the matrix to                 基準。本集團將調整矩陣，以就前瞻性資料對
     adjust the historical credit loss experience with forward-looking              過往信貸虧損經驗作出調整。例如，若預測經
     information. For instance, if forecast economic conditions (i.e., gross        濟狀況（即國內生產總值）於未來一年惡化，從
     domestic product) are expected to deteriorate over the next year               而導致印刷產品行業的違約數量增加，歷史違
     which can lead to an increased number of defaults in the printing              約率將予調整。於各報告日期，歷史觀察違約
     product sector, the historical default rates are adjusted. At each             率將會予以更新，並分析前瞻性估計的變化。
     reporting date, the historical observed default rates are updated and
     changes in the forward-looking estimates are analysed.

          by the Company on 21 February 2020. The purpose of the Scheme              劃（「該計劃」）。該計劃的目的在於容許本公司
          is to enable the Company to grant options to selected participants         向獲挑選的參與者授出購股權，作為其對本集

          Scheme will remain in force for 10 years from that date.                   起計10年內維持有效。

    An option may be accepted by a participant within 28 days from the               參與者可自授出購股權當日起計28日內接納購
    date of the grant of the option. The Directors may, at their absolute            股權。董事向參與者授出購股權時可全權酌情
    discretion, fix any minimum period for which an option must be                   釐定必須持有購股權的最短期間、任何必須達
    held, any performance targets that must be achieved and any other                致的表現目標及於購股權可予行使前必須達成
    conditions that must be fulfilled before the options can be exercised            的任何其他條件。
    upon the grant of an option to a participant.

    The subscription price of a Share in respect of any option granted               根據該計劃授出的任何購股權相關股份的認購
    under the Scheme, subject to any adjustments made in accordance                  價須由董事會全權酌情釐定（可根據該計劃作
    with the Scheme, shall be such price as the Board in its absolute                出任何調整），惟該價格不得低於下列各項的
    discretion shall determine, provided that such price shall not be                最高者：(i)股份於緊接授出購股權日期（必須為
    less than the highest of (i) the average closing price of the Shares             營業日（定義見上市規則））前五個營業日在聯
    as stated in the Stock Exchange’s daily quotations sheet for the                 交所每日報價表所示的平均收市價；(ii)股份於
    five business days (as defined in the Listing Rules) immediately                 授出購股權日期（必須為營業日（定義見上市規
    preceding the date of grant of the option (which must be a business              則））在聯交所每日報價表所示收市價；及(iii)
    day (as defined in the Listing Rules)); (ii) the closing price of the            股份的面值。接納授出購股權要約時須支付代
    Shares as stated in the Stock Exchange’s daily quotations sheet                  價1.00港元。
    on the date of grant of the option (which must be a business day
    (as defined in the Listing Rules)); and (iii) the nominal value of the
    Shares. A consideration of HK$1.00 is payable on acceptance of the
    offer of the grant of an option.

    There are no cash settlement alternatives. The Group does not have               並無現金結算替代方案。本集團過往並無就該
    a past practice of cash settlement for these share options. The                  等購股權進行現金結算的慣例。本集團將該計
    Group accounts for the Scheme as an equity-settled plan.                         劃視為一項股權結算計劃。

30. RESERVES                                                                  30. 儲備

    The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein for           本集團於本年度及過往年度的儲備及其變動金
    current and prior years are presented in the consolidated statement         額於財務報表第55頁的綜合權益變動表呈列。
    of changes in equity on page 55 of the financial statements.

    (a) Merger reserve                                                          (a) 合併儲備

         The merger reserve represents the aggregate of the paid-                  合併儲備指於重組前控股股東應佔現時組
         up share capital of the subsidiaries now comprising the                   成本集團的附屬公司之繳足股本總額。
         Group attributable to the Controlling Shareholders prior to the
         Reorganisation.

         The balance of the merger reserve at 31 December 2023                     於2023年及2022年12月31日的合併儲備
         and 2022 mainly represented the deemed distribution to the                結餘主要指根據重組透過發行本公司股份
         Controlling Shareholders by issuance of the Company’s shares              視作向控股股東的分派。
         pursuant to the Reorganisation.

    (b) Exchange fluctuation reserve                                            (b) 外匯波動儲備

         The exchange fluctuation reserve comprises all relevant                   外匯波動儲備包括換算海外業務財務報表
         exchange differences arising from the translation of the financial        而產生的所有相關匯兌差額。
         statements of foreign operations.

    (c) Share premium                                                           (c) 股份溢價

         The share premium represents the difference between the par               股份溢價指就重組而言已發行股份的面值
         value of the shares issued and the consideration paid for the             與已付代價之間的差額。
         Reorganisation.

          arise from sales or purchases by operating units in currencies other           單位使用單位功能貨幣以外的貨幣所進行之銷
          than the units’ functional currencies.                                         售或採購。

    Liquidity risk                                                                  流動資金風險

    The Group monitors its risk to a shortage of funds using a recurring            本集團使用經常性流動資金規劃工具監控其資
    liquidity planning tool. This tool considers the maturity of both its           金短缺風險。該工具計及其金融工具及金融資
    financial instruments and financial assets and projected cash flows             產兩者的到期日以及預測經營所得現金流量。
    from operations.

    The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance between continuity of            本集團的目標為透過使用銀行借款及營運所得
    funding and flexibility through the use of bank borrowings and funds            資金，在資金之可持續性及靈活度之間取得平
    generated from operations.                                                      衡。

          Capital management                                                         資本管理

          The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to            本集團進行資本管理主要旨在保障本集團的持
          safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order      續經營能力，以為股東提供回報及維持最佳的
          to provide returns for shareholders and to maintain an optimal capital     資本結構以減低資金成本。
          structure to reduce the cost of capital.

          The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments to           本集團管理其資本結構及因應經濟狀況變動作
          it in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or adjust       出調整。為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能
          the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend payment           會調整派付予股東的股息款項或向股東退還資
          to shareholders or return capital to shareholders. No changes were         本。截至2023年及2022年12月31日止年度，
          made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing capital         管理資本的目標、政策或程序並無出現變動。
          during the years ended 31 December 2023 and 2022.

          The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net debt        本集團使用資產負債比率（即淨債務除以資本
          divided by the total capital. Net debt includes interest-bearing           總額）監察資本。淨債務包括計息銀行借款及
          bank borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital represents the equity       租賃負債。資本指本公司擁有人應佔權益。於
          attributable to owners of the Company. The gearing ratios as at the        報告期末的資產負債比率如下：
          end of the reporting periods were as follows:
